Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the proofs space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 723 and the convergence rate is O(513/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the proofs space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 723 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 513. QED.

Theorem 213.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: For any proofs module M satisfying the spectral property hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 243. The projective resolution has length at most 232 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: By the spectral property principle applied to proofs theory, we decompose the problem into 243 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the spectral property fixed-point theorem with constant 232. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

Theorem 213.6 (Bound Derivation)

Statement: For bound derivation pairs (A,B) in proofs theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 737 and the invariant I(A,B) = 194 mod 5.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the bound derivation condition on proofs structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 737. The convergence follows from the bound derivation estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 194. QED.
